```turtle
            ] ;
            sh:property [

                sh:minCount 1 ;

                sh:message "Adjusted ice load force must equal ice load force multiplied by (1 - stringerOutsideIceBeltHeight / stringerSpan)." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:supportedStringerOutsideIceBelt true ;
                              nltl:iceLoadForce ?forceNode ;
                              nltl:stringerOutsideIceBeltHeight ?heightNode ;
                              nltl:stringerSpan ?spanNode ;
                              nltl:adjustedIceLoadForce ?adjustedNode .

                        ?forceNode qudt:numericValue ?force .
                        ?heightNode qudt:numericValue ?height .
                        ?spanNode qudt:numericValue ?span .
                        ?adjustedNode qudt:numericValue ?adjusted .

                        FILTER (?span != 0)
                        BIND (?force * (1 - (?height / ?span)) AS ?expected)

                        FILTER (?adjusted != ?expected)
                    }
                """
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Stringer span must be non-zero to evaluate the adjusted ice load force formula." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:supportedStringerOutsideIceBelt true ;
                              nltl:stringerSpan ?spanNode .
                        ?spanNode qudt:numericValue ?span .
                        FILTER (?span = 0)
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF_058_AdjustedIceLoad a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:webFrame ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:supportedStringerOutsideIceBelt ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:supportedStringerOutsideIceBelt ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceLoadForce ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:stringerOutsideIceBeltHeight ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:stringerSpan ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:adjustedIceLoadForce ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "adjustedIceLoadForce must equal iceLoadForce multiplied by (1 - stringerOutsideIceBeltHeight / stringerSpan), within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 and an absolute fallback of 1e-6." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:iceLoadForce ?forceNode ;
                              nltl:stringerOutsideIceBeltHeight ?heightNode ;
                              nltl:stringerSpan ?spanNode ;
                              nltl:adjustedIceLoadForce ?resultNode .
                        ?forceNode qudt:numericValue ?force .
                        ?heightNode qudt:numericValue ?height .
                        ?spanNode qudt:numericValue ?span .
                        ?resultNode qudt:numericValue ?result .
                        BIND (?force * (1 - (?height / ?span)) AS ?expected)
                        FILTER (
                            ?span = 0 ||
                            ABS(?result - ?expected) >
                            (ABS(?expected) * 0.000001 + 0.000001)
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:supportedStringerOutsideIceBelt ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

```turtle
                    SELECT $this
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF015Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:intendedRouteSeaWaterSalinity ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:SalinityQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:loadingCalculationSeaWaterSalinity ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:SalinityQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:TRF015IceNavigationShape
        gen:TRF015NonIceNavigationShape
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The loading-calculation sea-water salinity must equal the intended-route sea-water salinity." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:intendedRouteSeaWaterSalinity ?intended ;
                      nltl:loadingCalculationSeaWaterSalinity ?loading .
                ?intended qudt:numericValue ?intendedValue ;
                          qudt:unit nltl:gramPerKilogramSalinityUnit .
                ?loading qudt:numericValue ?loadingValue ;
                         qudt:unit nltl:gramPerKilogramSalinityUnit .
                FILTER (
                    datatype(?intendedValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?loadingValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    ?intendedValue != ?loadingValue
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "When navigating in ice, the operating draught and trim must not exceed their permitted limits." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:navigatingInIce true ;
                      nltl:operatingDraught ?operatingDraught ;
                      nltl:upperIceWaterlineDraught ?upperDraught ;
                      nltl:operatingTrim ?operatingTrim ;
                      nltl:maximumPermittedIceTrim ?maximumTrim .

                ?operatingDraught qudt:numericValue ?operatingDraughtValue ;
                                  qudt:unit unit:M .
                ?upperDraught qudt:numericValue ?upperDraughtValue ;
                              qudt:unit unit:M .
                ?operatingTrim qudt:numericValue ?operatingTrimValue ;
                               qudt:unit unit:M .
                ?maximumTrim qudt:numericValue ?maximumTrimValue ;
                             qudt:unit unit:M .

                FILTER (
                    datatype(?operatingDraughtValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?upperDraughtValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?operatingTrimValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?maximumTrimValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    (
                        ?operatingDraughtValue > ?upperDraughtValue ||
                        ?operatingTrimValue > ?maximumTrimValue
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:SalinityQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:gramPerKilogramSalinityUnit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .
```

```turtle
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarClassRequirementsApplicable ;

        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "polarClassRequirementsApplicable must be recorded exactly once."
    ] ;

        g:I2_046_ApplicableShape
        g:I2_046_NonApplicableShape

        sh:path nltl:polarClassRequirementsApplicable ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;

        sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarClassPc1
            nltl:polarClassPc2
            nltl:polarClassPc3
            nltl:polarClassPc4

        sh:path nltl:hasInternalIceStrengthenedStructure ;
        sh:node g:I2_046_InternalStructureShape
    ] .

g:I2_046_NonApplicableShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarClassRequirementsApplicable ;
        sh:hasValue false
    ] .

g:I2_046_InternalStructureShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:internalStructureCorrosionAbrasionAddition ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node g:I2_046_AdditionQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each linked internal ice-strengthened structure must have exactly one corrosion/abrasion addition."
    ] .

g:I2_046_AdditionQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:minInclusive "1.0"^^xsd:decimal ;
        sh:message "The corrosion/abrasion addition must be at least 1.0 mm."
    ] ;

        sh:maxCount 1 ;
```

```turtle
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:airReceiverConsecutiveStartCapacity ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:minInclusive 6
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "The propulsion astern-reversal state must be recorded, with at least 12 consecutive starts when reversal is required or at least 6 otherwise." .
```

```turtle
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:clampedHullGeometryTerm ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "For applicable IA or IA Super ships, clampedHullGeometryTerm must equal the clamped value of ((shipLength * iceClassDraught) / shipBreadth^2)^3, using relative tolerance 1e-6 with a small absolute fallback near zero." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:shipLength ?lengthNode ;
                              nltl:iceClassDraught ?draughtNode ;
                              nltl:shipBreadth ?breadthNode ;
                              nltl:clampedHullGeometryTerm ?resultNode .
                        ?lengthNode qudt:numericValue ?length .
                        ?draughtNode qudt:numericValue ?draught .
                        ?breadthNode qudt:numericValue ?breadth .
                        ?resultNode qudt:numericValue ?reported .
                        BIND((((?length * ?draught) / (?breadth * ?breadth)) *
                              ((?length * ?draught) / (?breadth * ?breadth)) *
                              ((?length * ?draught) / (?breadth * ?breadth))) AS ?raw)
                        BIND(IF(?raw < 5, 5, IF(?raw > 20, 20, ?raw)) AS ?expected)
                        FILTER (
                            ABS(?reported - ?expected) >
                            ((0.000001 * ABS(?expected)) + 0.000000001)
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:minInclusive "2003-09-01"^^xsd:date
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
```

```turtle
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

nltl:TRF109ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minInclusive 5000000 ;
        sh:maxInclusive 100000000 ;
        sh:message "The ice load cycle count must be between 5000000 and 100000000 inclusive."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasTableLookupCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node nltl:TRF109TableLookupCaseShape ;
        sh:message "A table lookup case using TRAFICOM Table 6-14 is required."
    ] .

nltl:TRF109TableLookupCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:tableLookupCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:tableReference ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
```

```turtle
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;

        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:additionalEquipmentCarriedInSurvivalCraft ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Applicability must indicate that additional equipment is carried in the survival craft."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:personsCapacityRequirement ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The persons capacity requirement is required when additional equipment is carried."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:additionalEquipmentCapacityRequirement ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The additional equipment capacity requirement is required when additional equipment is carried."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:survivalCraftAvailableCapacity ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The available survival-craft capacity is required when additional equipment is carried."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:loadedSurvivalCraftRequirement ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:KiloGM ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The loaded survival-craft requirement must be a single kilogram quantity."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:launchingApplianceCapacity ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:KiloGM ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The launching-appliance capacity must be a single kilogram quantity."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Survival-craft capacity must be at least the persons capacity requirement plus the additional equipment capacity requirement, and launching-appliance capacity must be at least the loaded survival-craft requirement." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:personsCapacityRequirement ?persons ;
                              nltl:additionalEquipmentCapacityRequirement ?additional ;
                              nltl:survivalCraftAvailableCapacity ?available ;
                              nltl:loadedSurvivalCraftRequirement ?loaded ;
                              nltl:launchingApplianceCapacity ?launching .
                        ?loaded qudt:numericValue ?loadedValue .
                        ?launching qudt:numericValue ?launchingValue .
                        FILTER (
                            ?available < (?persons + ?additional) ||
                            ?launchingValue < ?loadedValue
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]

            sh:property [
```

```turtle
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this

                $this nltl:personsCapacityRequirement ?persons ;
```

```turtle
    a sh:NodeShape ;

                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;

                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The additional-equipment applicability indicator must be true for the applicable capacity calculation."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:personsCapacityRequirement ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The persons capacity requirement is required when additional equipment is carried."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:additionalEquipmentCapacityRequirement ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The additional equipment capacity requirement is required when additional equipment is carried."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:loadedSurvivalCraftRequirement ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:KiloGM ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "The loaded survival craft requirement must be provided as a mass in kilograms."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:survivalCraftAvailableCapacity ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The available survival craft capacity is required when additional equipment is carried."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:launchingApplianceCapacity ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:KiloGM ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1

                sh:message "Survival craft capacity must be at least the persons capacity requirement plus the additional equipment capacity requirement, and launching appliance capacity must be at least the loaded survival craft requirement." ;

PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT ?this
WHERE {
    ?this nltl:personsCapacityRequirement ?persons .
    ?this nltl:additionalEquipmentCapacityRequirement ?additional .

        || ?launching < ?loaded
    )
}
"""
```

Original source text:
Regard shall be paid to the deflection of the ship's sides due to ice pressure with respect to very long (more than B/2) hatch openings, when designing weatherdeck hatch covers and their fittings.
Normalized requirement:

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF056ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasWeatherdeckHatch ;
        sh:node gen:TRF056WeatherdeckHatchShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipBreadth ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "A ship with a weatherdeck hatch must provide ship breadth in metres." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasWeatherdeckHatch ?hatch .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:shipBreadth ?breadthValue .
                    ?breadthValue qudt:numericValue ?breadth .
                    ?breadthValue qudt:unit unit:M .
                    FILTER (datatype(?breadth) = xsd:decimal)
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a hatch opening longer than half the ship breadth, ship-side deflection and both hatch-cover and hatch-fitting design evidence must be represented." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasWeatherdeckHatch ?hatch .
                $this nltl:shipBreadth ?breadthValue .
                ?breadthValue qudt:numericValue ?breadth .
                ?breadthValue qudt:unit unit:M .
                ?hatch nltl:hatchOpeningLength ?openingValue .
                ?openingValue qudt:numericValue ?opening .
                ?openingValue qudt:unit unit:M .
                FILTER (datatype(?breadth) = xsd:decimal)
                FILTER (datatype(?opening) = xsd:decimal)
                FILTER (?opening > (?breadth / 2))
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?hatch nltl:shipSideDeflection ?deflection
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?hatch nltl:hasHatchCoverDesignEvidence ?coverEvidence
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?hatch nltl:hasHatchFittingDesignEvidence ?fittingEvidence
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:TRF056WeatherdeckHatchShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hatchOpeningLength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipSideDeflection ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasHatchCoverDesignEvidence ;
        sh:class nltl:hatchCoverDesignEvidence
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasHatchFittingDesignEvidence ;
        sh:class nltl:hatchFittingDesignEvidence
    ] .
```
